- **odin-ingest**: Odin's bulk-ingest worker (ADR-0045). Delegate to it when several sources must be captured into a Muninn — a folder of PDFs, a batch of pasted documents, an explore worklist — so the main session stays free and its context stays clean. Requires the Muninn root and an explicit source list; it will refuse rather than guess either. Runs capture → derive per source and reports ids, hashes, and per-source outcomes. ([WillCAboutThat/odin-adapter](https://github.com/WillCAboutThat/odin-adapter/blob/HEAD/odin/agents/odin-ingest.md) · ★1)
- **odin-map**: Odin's map-discovery worker (ADR-0045; the T-253 rail discipline). Delegate the map verb's discovery reads to it: it grounds entity/concept/question candidates in actual source bytes and STAGES each one on the candidates rail as it is confirmed - so a lost worker strands minutes, never the pass. It never writes derived docs; the manifest is assembled from the rail in the main session, and promotion is the operator's one nod. Requires the Muninn root and the resolved scope; it refuses rather than guesses either. ([WillCAboutThat/odin-adapter](https://github.com/WillCAboutThat/odin-adapter/blob/HEAD/odin/agents/odin-map.md) · ★1)

- **okra-challenger**: First-principles review agent for OKRA introspection (stage 2). Use at introspection trigger junctures — DKR checkpoint acceptance, flag opening, level descent, terminalization, or the every-3rd-check-in heartbeat — whenever a live hypothesis needs an independent intact/drifted/dead verdict. It restates the objective from the frame (never from momentum), interrogates each spine hypothesis against fresh evidence, and returns verdict-as-evidence with refs; it never edits files, never steers, never talks to the human, and its verdict is input to the orchestrator, not a decision. ([lagz0ne/okra](https://github.com/lagz0ne/okra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/okra-challenger.md))
- **okra-orchestrator**: OKRA loop steering agent. Use for check-in rounds, admissibility screening before dispatching work, reading run-store state, deciding the next move, appending check-ins/flags/metric reads, and composing worker prompt packets. It never executes product work itself and never edits the frame — it steers inside it. ([lagz0ne/okra](https://github.com/lagz0ne/okra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/okra-orchestrator.md))
- **okra-validator**: Deterministic verification agent for OKRA work. Use after any change to the skill, checkers, evals, runner, or run store — or whenever a claim ("anti-goal held", "checker passes", "artifact complete", "done") needs to become an executable audit trace instead of an assertion. It replays evidence mechanically and reports accepted/rejected decisions with hashes; it does not judge prose quality and does not fix anything. ([lagz0ne/okra](https://github.com/lagz0ne/okra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/okra-validator.md))
- **okra-writer**: Deep-work execution agent for this repository — progresses the actual work: editing the reverse-tornado-okr skill, references, contracts, blindbox checkers, golden corpora, cases, fixtures, and the runner. Use for any DKR probe or PKR/task dispatched by the orchestrator, or any non-trivial implementation the user asks for. It executes within its packet scope, hands back on unknowns, and never validates its own claims. ([lagz0ne/okra](https://github.com/lagz0ne/okra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/okra-writer.md))
- **okta-auth-specialist**: Okta/Auth0 OIDC specialist implementing the BFF pattern — auth-code + PKCE, server-side sessions, token exchange, step-up MFA, and downstream authorization policies. Use for any login, session, scope, claims, or authorization work. ([MeridianLZ/copilot-plugins](https://github.com/MeridianLZ/copilot-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/fintech-backend/agents/okta-auth-specialist.md) · 2 copies)
